### [1] [LogCopilot: Automating Log Aggregation Analysis through Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17094)
*Senyu Xie,Chenxi Zhang,Tong Zhou,Jiacheng Liu,Xiaoyu Hong,Qingshan Li,Xin Peng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出LogCopilot，一种基于大语言模型的自动化日志聚合分析框架，通过知识检索和工具调用，将自然语言指令转化为LogQL查询，在四个数据集上平均准确率达76.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前工业系统中，工程师使用DSL查询语言编写日志分析查询耗时费力，需要深入理解DSL语法和日志细节，缺乏自动化的自然语言到查询的转换方法。

Method: LogCopilot包含三个核心组件：1) 层次化知识库，用于表示和检索日志关键知识；2) 基于LLM的自然语言指令理解与查询生成模块；3) 工具调用机制，自动生成并执行LogQL查询，完成日志聚合分析。

Result: 在四个日志数据集上平均准确率达76.8%，优于基线方法；实验结果表明LogCopilot在LogQL查询生成任务上表现有效。

Conclusion: LogCopilot通过结合大语言模型与层次化知识库，有效实现了从自然语言到日志聚合查询的自动化转换，降低了日志分析的门槛和人力成本。

Abstract: Logs record the runtime behavior of software and are widely used in various tasks such as debugging, testing, and fault diagnosis. With the increase in system size and complexity, log analysis has gradually become a challenging task. Current industrial systems typically use log aggregation systems such as Grafana Loki and ELK to simplify the log collection and analysis process. Engineers write queries using the DSL query language provided by these systems can complete a variety of log analysis tasks. However, writing these queries is often time-consuming and labor-intensive, as it requires engineers to have a thorough understanding of the DSL syntax and the detailed information contained in the logs. To address these challenges, this paper proposes LogCopilot, an automated log aggregation analysis framework based on large language models (LLMs). LogCopilot accepts natural language log analysis instructions and accomplishes automated log analysis through knowledge retrieval and tool calling. LogCopilot constructs a hierarchical knowledge base to represent and provide key knowledge in logs. And it achieves automated log aggregation analysis by generating and executing LogQL queries. The evaluation based on four log datasets confirm the effectiveness of LogCopilot, which achieves an average accuracy of 76.8% and outperforms baseline approaches. Moreover, experiment results shows that LogCopilot is effective in LogQL query generation.

</details>


### [2] [Software Delegation Contracts: Measuring Reviewability in AI Coding-Agent Work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17099)
*Vincent Schmalbach*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过64次agent执行和192次评审的对照实验，发现显式委托合同并未提升编码agent的任务正确性（全部通过验收测试），但显著改善了可审查性：证据充分性提升（p<0.0001, Cliff's delta=0.66），评审歧义降低（p=0.035），代价是+13% token消耗和+38%耗时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究提出了软件委托合同作为编码代理工作的分析单元，但尚未测量其实际效果。本研究旨在通过对照实验量化显式委托合同对编码agent任务表现的影响。

Method: 构建无依赖的TypeScript API任务环境，包含种子缺陷和文档漏洞；设计10个任务（5个家族）；在两种模型层级下，进行64次agent执行，分为三组：现实issue式提示、显式委托合同、带证据捆绑的合同；使用隐藏验收测试、变异检查和范围分析进行评分；由3位条件盲化的模型评审员按固定评分标准进行192次评审。

Result: 所有64次执行均通过隐藏验收测试，零范围违规。显式合同未提升客观任务结果。但显著改善了可审查性：证据充分性在30组配对比较中22组提升、0组下降（+0.83/5分，p<0.0001, Cliff's delta=0.66）；评审歧义降低（p=0.035）；变更文件列表、已知限制、残余风险部分和评审检查表大多仅在合同要求时出现。合同带来+13% token和+38%时间开销，对较弱模型影响更大。

Conclusion: 在小型任务上，委托合同购买的是可审查性而非正确性。显式合同能结构化agent输出以提升人工评审效率，但需权衡额外计算成本。

Abstract: AI coding agents increasingly accept assigned software tasks, modify repositories under bounded authority, and return work packages for review. Prior work proposed the software delegation contract, covering the task, authority, returned work package, and acceptance context, as the unit of analysis for delegated coding work, but did not measure its effects. This paper reports a controlled pilot study of explicit delegation contracts for coding agents. We built a dependency-free TypeScript API task environment with seeded defects and documentation gaps, authored ten tasks across five families, and ran 64 agent executions across two model tiers under three conditions: a realistic issue-style prompt, an explicit delegation contract, and a contract with a required evidence bundle. Each run was scored with hidden acceptance tests, mutation checks, and scope analysis, then reviewed by three independent condition-blinded model-based reviewers using a fixed rubric, for 192 reviews. Explicit contracts did not improve objective task outcomes: all 64 runs passed hidden acceptance checks, with zero scope violations. They did improve reviewability. Evidence sufficiency improved in 22 of 30 paired comparisons and worsened in none (+0.83 on a 5-point scale, p < 0.0001, Cliff's delta = 0.66); reviewer ambiguity decreased (p = 0.035); changed-file lists, known-limitations sections, residual-risk sections, and reviewer checklists appeared mostly or only when demanded by the contract. Contracts cost +13% agent tokens and +38% wall-clock time, with larger effects for the weaker model tier. On these small tasks, delegation contracts bought reviewability rather than correctness.

</details>


### [3] [Cluster-Aware Dual-Level Test Specification Generation for Large-Scale Automotive Software Requirements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17197)
*Hazem Ayman,Menna Sedik,Kareem Mostafa,Mahmoud Soliman,Samer Saber,Ibrahim Habib*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"先聚类再总结"的三阶段流水线，通过嵌入、降维聚类、多级Map-Reduce摘要及RAG增强，在汽车级数千条需求规模下自动生成同时覆盖单需求验证和跨需求集成测试的测试规范，比基线方法显著提升集成测试覆盖率并保持摘要保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着汽车项目需求规模增长到数千条，人工生成满足Automotive SPICE SWE.6要求的测试规范耗时数周，手动过程不可持续；而标准LLM方法存在两个关键问题：逐个处理需求会丢失需求间依赖关系，整体输入又会超出上下文窗口限制，导致集成覆盖不完整和测试用例冗余。

Method: 三阶段流水线：1) 使用Sentence Transformers嵌入需求，经UMAP降维后用HDBSCAN进行密度聚类，并通过自动最小聚类大小选择（结合归一化Silhouette和Calinski-Harabasz分数）优化聚类质量；2) 采用多级Map-Reduce摘要算法将每个聚类提炼为符合领域规范、保留定量阈值和安全完整性等级的简洁描述；3) 利用聚类拓扑生成两级测试规范——单需求验证测试和簇级跨需求集成测试，并通过邻近簇上下文机制在LLM调用中提供有限跨特征感知，同时用RAG将输出锚定在ISO 26262和ASPICE标准上。

Result: 在多个规模不同的汽车需求数据集上，该聚类感知方法相比基线方法显著提升了集成测试覆盖率，同时保持了摘要保真度，并能高效扩展到数千条需求规模。

Conclusion: "Cluster-then-Summarize"流水线通过聚类驱动的方式同时解决了需求间依赖捕获与上下文窗口限制的矛盾，为大规模汽车软件测试规范自动生成提供了一种可扩展、高质量的有效方案。

Abstract: Generating test specifications that satisfy Automotive SPICE SWE.6 requirements becomes increasingly challenging and time-consuming as projects scale to thousands of requirements. Because this manual process often consumes weeks of engineering effort, automation becomes a critical necessity. However, standard Large Language Model (LLM) approaches struggle at scale: processing requirements individually discards vital inter-requirement dependencies, while feeding entire corpora at once exceeds context-window limits, leading to incomplete integration coverage and redundant test cases. This paper presents a novel "Cluster-then-Summarize" pipeline that addresses these limitations through three-stages. Requirements are embedded using sentence transformers and grouped using UMAP dimensionality reduction followed by HDBSCAN density-based clustering. This grouping utilizes an automatic minimum cluster size selection driven by a quality criterion combining normalized Silhouette and Calinski-Harabasz scores. A multi-level map-reduce summarization algorithm then distills each cluster into concise, domain-conformant descriptions while preserving quantitative thresholds and safety integrity levels. The pipeline exploits the derived cluster topology to generate test specifications at two levels: individual requirement verification and cluster-level integration tests that verify cross-requirement feature behavior. A nearby-cluster context mechanism provides bounded cross-feature awareness during each LLM call, and Retrieval-Augmented Generation grounds all outputs in ISO 26262 and ASPICE standards. Evaluation on automotive requirement datasets of varying scale demonstrates that the cluster-aware approach improves integration test coverage and maintains summarization fidelity compared to baseline methods while scaling efficiently to thousands of requirements.

</details>


### [4] [Trust-Aware Multi-Agent Traceability: Confidence-Calibrated Knowledge Graphs for Consistent Software Artifact Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17203)
*Mohamed Essam,Kareem Wael,Azza Hassan,Ahmed Haitham,Mahmoud Soliman,Samer Saber,Ibrahim Habib*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于信任感知的协调框架，通过共享知识图谱和置信度评分机制，解决多智能体AI系统在软件工程流水线中错误传播和低置信度决策导致的问题，并在汽车软件工程案例中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体AI系统在软件工程任务中作为顺序流水线运行时，上游代理的错误和低置信度决策会传播到下游阶段，导致孤儿需求、矛盾链接和合规性缺口，这在安全关键领域构成重大风险。

Method: 提出信任感知协调框架：(1) 共享知识图谱作为集中语义记忆和协调曲面；(2) 两阶段可追溯性链接预测流水线（嵌入检索+LLM多标准分析）；(3) 可追溯性种子机制（比较推导时与验证时的置信度）；(4) 一致性协议（置信度阈值门控、置信度发散检测、冲突解决）。

Result: 在汽车软件工程案例研究中评估了链接预测校准、协议有效性、阈值敏感性和可追溯性种子的影响。消融研究证实置信度校准对有效流水线协调至关重要。

Conclusion: 信任感知协调框架能有效缓解多智能体流水线中的错误传播问题，置信度校准是确保流水线协调有效性的关键因素。

Abstract: Multi-agent AI systems are increasingly used to automate software engineering tasks including requirements analysis, architecture design, test generation, and traceability linking. When these agents operate as a sequential pipeline over shared software artifacts, errors and low-confidence decisions made by upstream agents propagate to downstream stages, producing orphaned requirements, contradictory links, and compliance gaps that pose significant risks in safety-critical domains. We propose a trust-aware coordination framework where a shared knowledge graph serves as both centralized semantic memory and a coordination surface through which agents assess and build upon each other's contributions using calibrated confidence scores. Our approach introduces a two-stage traceability link prediction pipeline combining embedding-based retrieval with LLM-based multi-criteria analysis, a traceability seeding mechanism that enables comparison between derivation-time and validation-time confidence, and a consistency protocol governing pipeline interactions through confidence threshold gating, confidence divergence detection, and conflict resolution. We evaluate on an automotive software engineering case study measuring link prediction calibration, protocol effectiveness, threshold sensitivity, and the impact of traceability seeding. Ablation studies confirm that confidence calibration is essential for effective pipeline coordination.

</details>


### [5] [Supporting the Adoption of Privacy-Enhancing Technologies through Requirements Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17387)
*Oleksandr Kosenkov,Vadym Honcharenko,Abhinava Singh,Volodymyr Spirin,Danica Vranjanin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文从需求工程视角分析了隐私增强技术（PETs）在软件工程中采用率低的跨学科挑战（工程、法律、商业），并论证系统化的RE可协调多方利益相关者以促进PETs的实际应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管PETs在学术界和工业界受到广泛关注，但其在实际软件工程中的采用率仍然有限，且已知的挑战（如技术复杂性、培训不足）长期未得到解决。

Method: 采用工业挑战论文形式，从需求工程视角出发，跨多个利益相关者群体（PET开发者、集成者、采用者）和多个学科视角（工程、法律、商业）系统分析PETs采用面临的挑战。

Result: 识别出PETs采用需同时考虑工程、商业和法律三个互补视角；任一视角的忽视（如对软件架构的影响、商业影响、监管合规贡献）都会增加采用障碍甚至导致实施失败。

Conclusion: 在需求工程中明确规范这三个视角，能够实现利益相关者之间的有效协调，从而在软件工程中更有效地实现PETs的收益。

Abstract: In recent decades, privacy-enhancing technologies (PETs) have been recognized as a means of meeting regulatory and user privacy requirements in software systems that process personal data. Despite substantial research efforts, support from regulators, contributions by large technology companies such as Google and Microsoft, and growing interest among software practitioners, the practical adoption of PETs remains limited. Existing research consistently identifies recurring challenges to PETs adoption in SE, such as technical complexity and insufficient training. Despite ongoing research efforts, these challenges largely remain unresolved in practice.
  In this industrial challenge paper, we apply a practical, requirements engineering (RE)-driven perspective to examine challenges to PET adoption across multiple stakeholder groups (PET developers, integrators, and adopters) as well as across different disciplinary perspectives (engineering, law, and business).
  We argue that RE can facilitate the adoption of PETs by systematically addressing each of the complementary engineering, business, and legal viewpoints on privacy. Neglecting challenges in any of these viewpoints (e.g., the impact of PETs on software architecture, their business implications, and their contribution to regulatory compliance) can increase the impediments or even lead to implementation failure. In practice, explicit specification of these viewpoints within RE can enable meaningful coordination among stakeholders to more effectively realize the benefits of PETs in software engineering.

</details>


### [6] [OmniDroneX: An LLM-Assisted Holistic Drone-as-a-Service Ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17510)
*I-Ling Yen,Akeem Mohammed,Farokh Bastani,San-Yih Hwang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: OmniDroneX提出了一个统一的无人机即服务（Drone-as-a-Service）生态系统，通过LLM驱动的分层架构和多种服务组合技术，将无人机从固定功能平台转变为动态可组合实体，实现可扩展、自演化的无人机系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前无人机技术虽快速发展，但系统研究存在多项空白（gaps），导致实际部署受限；现有无人机平台功能固定，缺乏动态可组合性和与外部基础设施的集成能力。

Method: 提出OmniDroneX生态系统的三层核心方法：(1) 统一供应商无关接口libUAV和形式化物理服务抽象模型PT-SOA，桥接底层物理基元与高层任务意图；(2) 将LLM跨层应用于设备功能识别、服务定义形式化、自动化服务组合和自然语言任务规约；(3) 引入多种组合技术，包括物理层组合、时空组合、功能组合、协作组合、异常感知组合和QoS组合。

Result: OmniDroneX能够作为可扩展、弹性、自演化的无人机生态系统的基础，适用于复杂动态环境。

Conclusion: OmniDroneX通过将无人机从固定功能平台转变为动态可组合实体，结合LLM驱动和多种服务组合技术，为构建下一代智能无人机系统提供了统一架构基础。

Abstract: Despite rapid advances in UAV technologies, current deployments remain limited due to several gaps in UAV systems research. To address these challenges, we propose OmniDroneX, a unified Drone-as-a-Service ecosystem, in which drones are transitioned from fixed function platforms into dynamically composable entities that can be integrated with external infrastructures to offer omni-capabilities. OmniDroneX bridges low-level physical primitives with high-level mission intent through a unified vendor-agnostic interface (libUAV) and a formal physical-service abstraction model (PT-SOA). A core innovation is the diverse application of large language models (LLMs) across multiple layers of the OmniDroneX architecture. LLMs are used to assist in identifying and formalizing primitive device functions and abstract service definitions, supporting automated service composition and workflow generation, and enabling interactive, natural-language mission specification and refinement. OmniDroneX also incorporates important categories of composition techniques that are essential in dynamic UAV systems, including physical layer composition for drone capability augmentation, as well as spatiotemporal, functional, collaborative, exception-aware, and QoS-based service compositions. Collectively, these features allow OmniDroneX to serve as a foundation for scalable, resilient, and self-evolving UAV ecosystems operating in complex and dynamic environments.

</details>


### [7] [Unlocking LLM Code Correction with Iterative Feedback Loops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17514)
*Le Zhang,Suresh Kothari*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了LLM通过执行反馈迭代修正自身代码的能力，发现推理模型在利用反馈方面显著优于非推理模型，语法和运行时错误比逻辑或算法错误更容易修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码生成评测多关注单次准确率，忽视了真实编程中迭代精炼的过程，缺乏对LLM通过执行反馈自我纠错能力的系统研究。

Method: 使用迭代精炼框架，LLM在每次尝试后接收编译器错误信息和测试用例反馈，在四种模型和两种编程语言上进行评估，并引入指标评估代码失败、分析修正模式、比较推理与非推理模型。

Result: 推理模型在迭代过程中持续改进，显著优于非推理模型；语法和运行时错误比逻辑或算法错误更容易被修正。

Conclusion: 反馈循环在LLM驱动的代码生成中具有重要作用，推理模型能更好地利用执行反馈，但逻辑和算法层面的失败仍是更具挑战性的问题。

Abstract: Large Language Models have shown remarkable capabilities in code generation. However, most existing evaluations focus only on single-attempt accuracy and overlook the iterative refinement process that is central to real-world programming. This study presents a systematic investigation of LLMs' ability to rectify their own code through execution feedback. Using real-world programming problems across four models and two major programming languages, this study evaluates performance using iterative refinement framework where LLMs receive compiler error messages and testcase feedback after each attempt. This study introduces metrics to evaluate code failures, analyze rectification patterns, and compare the effectiveness of reasoning and non-reasoning models, offering actionable insights into both the understanding and practical application of feedback loops in LLM-driven code generation systems. Results show that reasoning models consistently improve over iterations, substantially outperforming non-reasoning models in leveraging feedback, while syntactic and runtime errors are far more tractable than logical or algorithmic failures.

</details>


### [8] [Understanding LLMs in Title-Abstract Screening: From Disagreements to Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17588)
*Mika Mäntylä,Patricia Matsubara,Katia Romero Felizardo,Miikka Kuutila,Marco Gerosa,Savio de Sousa Sampaio,Tayana Conte,Igor Steinmacher*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究超越定量分析，通过定性方法探究LLM在系统综述标题-摘要筛选中为何以及如何出错，并提出可操作建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注LLM与人类在系统综述筛选中的定量一致性（如Kappa值），但可靠性问题仍未解决，缺乏对LLM失败原因的深入理解。

Method: 对6项软件工程系统综述、超过1000篇论文进行独立双盲筛选（人类专家与零样本LLM），计算Kappa值（0.52-0.77），并对人机分歧进行定性分析。

Result: 人机分歧源于可识别、可复现的原因，包括：关键术语边界模糊、关键词过度强调、错误主题推断。

Conclusion: 建议在部署前验证语义理解能力、使用多个LLM并行筛选、将验证资源聚焦于边界案例；呼吁社区制定LLM在系统综述中使用的规范性指南。

Abstract: Several studies have examined the use of large language models (LLMs) for title-abstract screening in systematic reviews (SRs), reporting mixed accuracy. However, questions of reliability remain largely unaddressed. In this study, we go beyond quantitative LLM-human agreement metrics and qualitatively investigate how and why LLMs fail. We also propose actionable recommendations. We analyzed disagreements between LLMs and researchers across six software engineering SRs and over 1,000 primary study papers. For each SR, papers were screened independently by human experts and LLMs in zero-shot mode, resulting in Kappa values ranging from 0.52 to 0.77. Qualitative analysis suggests that human-LLM disagreement results from recurring, identifiable causes, such as boundary ambiguity in key terms, keyword overemphasization, and incorrect topic inference. Based on these findings, we propose recommendations such as validating semantic understanding before deployment, running multiple LLMs, and focusing validation efforts on borderline cases. Future studies are needed to validate the impact of our recommendations, and community efforts are needed to develop normative guidelines on LLM usage in SRs.

</details>


### [9] [Why Model Credibility Isn't Enough: -Rethinking Trust in Simulation Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17593)
*Romain Barbedienne,Adeline Lanugue,Rim Kaddah,Julien Silande,Anthony Levillain,Cedric Leclerc,Maxime Hayet,Boussaad Soualmi,Cristian Maxim*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文探讨了仿真架构可信度的评估问题，比较了敏感性分析、专家定性分析、AI可解释性和网络方法等四种用于评估组装可信度的技术，并基于严谨性、泛化性和资源需求等标准对各方法进行了优劣势分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿真模型的可信度已得到广泛研究，但仿真架构通常由多个模型组装而成，其整体可信度能否基于组成模型的可信度进行评估尚未明确。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 通过文献综述对比四种组装可信度评估方法：敏感性分析技术、专家定性分析、AI可解释性以及网络方法，并从严谨性、泛化性和资源需求等维度进行评估。

Result: 对四种方法在组装可信度评估中的优劣势进行了比较分析，揭示了各方法在严谨性、泛化能力和资源消耗等方面的差异。

Conclusion: 基于所提出的评估标准，给出了各方法的综合评估，为仿真架构组装可信度的评估提供了参考框架。

Abstract: Credibility of a simulation model is an important topic. Several approaches try to quantify the credibility of simulation. However, models are mostly assembled within a simulation architecture. Can the credibility of a simulation architecture be assessed based on the credibility of the models that comprise it? This paper aims to address this issue by providing an overview of the current state of the art in the field of assembly credibility. It will compare sensitivity analysis techniques, qualitative analysis by experts, explainability in AI, and networks. Finally, an assessment of the proposed approaches, based on criteria such as rigor, generalization, and resource requirements, will reveal the strengths and weaknesses of each approach.

</details>


### [10] [PracRepair: LLM-Empowered Automated Program Repair Inspired by Human-Like Debugging Practices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17612)
*Yu Cheng,Zhongxin Liu,Zhenchang Xing,Chao Ni,Qing Huang,Xiaoxue Ren*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: PracRepair是一个受人类调试实践启发、基于LLM的自动化程序修复框架，通过构建按需静态-动态上下文、执行问题驱动的故障诊断形成显式修复假设，并利用验证诊断和轨迹级行为变化迭代优化候选补丁。在Defects4J V1.2/V2.0上，GPT-3.5下正确修复139/136个bug，GPT-4o下提升至162/171，并在真实世界bug上取得最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的自动化程序修复方法主要依赖静态或检索上下文、错误消息和粗粒度的验证结果，未能充分利用动态信息（包括故障执行动态和补丁验证动态）来理解失败和进行修复。然而，故障执行轨迹规模大且噪声多、原始静态-动态上下文缺乏自解释性、补丁验证动态常被简化为粗粒度反馈，这些挑战使得有效利用动态信息变得困难。

Method: PracRepair框架包含三个核心步骤：1）从错误程序和故障执行中构建按需的静态-动态上下文；2）执行问题驱动的故障诊断，形成显式的修复假设；3）利用验证诊断和轨迹级行为变化，迭代优化候选补丁。该框架模拟人类调试的实践方式，充分利用动态信息指导修复过程。

Result: 在Defects4J V1.2和V2.0上，基于GPT-3.5时PracRepair分别正确修复139和136个bug；基于GPT-4o时提升至162和171个bug，一致优于现有最先进基线方法。此外，PracRepair在真实世界bug（RWB）数据集上也取得最佳性能，且效果在多个基础模型上具有泛化性。

Conclusion: PracRepair证明了充分利用动态信息（故障执行动态和补丁验证动态）能够显著提升LLM在自动化程序修复任务上的表现，其受人类调试启发的设计范式有效解决了现有方法的局限性，并在多个基准和模型上展现了优越的性能和泛化能力。

Abstract: As software systems grow in scale and complexity, debugging and repair remain costly and time-consuming. Large language models (LLMs) have advanced automated program repair (APR), but existing LLM-based APR approaches still largely rely on static or retrieved context, error messages, and coarse-grained validation outcomes. As a result, they underutilize dynamic information for failure understanding and repair, including failure-execution dynamics and patch-validation dynamics. Effectively leveraging such information, however, is challenging: failure-execution traces are large and noisy, raw static-dynamic context is not self-explanatory, and patch-validation dynamics are often reduced to coarse feedback. To address these challenges, we propose \textsc{PracRepair}, a fully automated LLM-based APR framework inspired by human-like debugging practices. \textsc{PracRepair} constructs an on-demand static-dynamic context from buggy programs and failure executions, performs question-driven failure diagnosis to formulate explicit repair hypotheses, and iteratively refines candidate patches using validation diagnostics and trace-level behavioral changes. Experimental results on Defects4J V1.2 and V2.0 show that \textsc{PracRepair} consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. Specifically, under GPT-3.5, \textsc{PracRepair} correctly fixes 139/136 bugs on Defects4J V1.2/V2.0, while under GPT-4o it further improves to 162/171. Moreover, \textsc{PracRepair} generalizes effectively to RWB (Real-World Bugs), achieving the best performance across multiple foundation models.

</details>


### [11] [FacProcessTwin: An LLM-Based System for Process Twin Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17666)
*Yash Pulse,Yong-Bin Kang,Abhik Banerjee,Prem Prakash Jayaraman*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出FacProcessTwin系统，利用大语言模型从工厂文档和操作员自然语言输入中自动构建过程孪生模型，并将其绑定到实时运行数据。通过在澳大利亚食品制造商的16个生产流程上的评估，该系统在模型生成上达到95.2%的平均F1值，开发时间约为人工的1/6，并通过人在回路机制消除了安全关键绑定中的错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过程孪生能够实时表示整个生产过程，具有推动整体效率提升的潜力，但开发成本高昂，需要精确建模整个生产过程及其设备、产品设置和工艺变化，还需绑定实时数据。现有方法开发耗时，亟需降低开发时间。

Method: FacProcessTwin利用大语言模型，从工厂过程文档和操作员自然语言输入中自动生成完整的过程模型，并将过程步骤自动绑定到实时运行数据，最终以交互式过程图呈现。系统支持人在回路的治理机制，操作员可监控和纠正系统的自主决策，特别是在安全关键绑定步骤中解决不确定性。

Result: 在澳大利亚食品制造商的16个生产流程上评估，覆盖冷藏、冷冻和无菌常温产品类别及同产品内的工艺变体。模型生成平均F1值为95.2%，构建每个孪生的时间约为人工的1/6。在安全关键绑定中，单次基线在模糊标签上75.0%出错，而FacProcessTwin通过交由操作员决策实现了零错误绑定。

Conclusion: FacProcessTwin通过大语言模型显著降低了过程孪生的开发时间和成本，同时保持了高准确率，其人在回路机制有效保证了安全关键绑定的正确性，为过程孪生的实际工业部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Process twins provide real-time representations of entire production processes. By capturing how process steps interact, rather than monitoring a single machine in isolation as an asset-based digital twin does, they have the potential to drive efficiency gains across the whole process. However, developing a process twin is costly. It requires accurately modelling the entire production process: its process steps, the equipment and product-specific settings each step uses, and its process variations. The resulting model must then be bound to live operational data. We present FacProcessTwin, a system that leverages a large language model (LLM) to reduce this development time, building a process twin from a plant's process documentation and natural-language input from an operator. FacProcessTwin generates this complete process model and then automatically binds its process steps to live operational data. The generated model and its data bindings are rendered as an interactive process diagram through which manufacturing personnel can monitor and correct the system's autonomous decisions, such as resolving uncertainty at safety-critical binding steps. We evaluate FacProcessTwin through a real-world case study of an Australian food manufacturer, covering 16 production process flows that span chilled, frozen, and aseptic shelf-stable product categories and include process variations within the same product. The results show that FacProcessTwin generates these process models accurately (a mean F1 of 95.2% against ground truth) and builds each twin in roughly a sixth of the manual time. Its human-in-the-loop governance then keeps the safety-critical bindings correct: at ambiguous tags where a single-pass baseline silently mis-binds 75.0% of the time, FacProcessTwin defers to the operator and mis-binds none.

</details>


### [12] [Position: Coding Benchmarks Are Misaligned with Agentic Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17799)
*Maria I. Gorinova,Macey Baker,Amy Heineike,Maksim Shaposhnikov,Rob Willoughby,Dru Knox*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 当前代码智能体基准测试将模型、框架和环境混为一谈，只给出单一端到端分数，与实际的智能体软件工程实践脱节，无法提供组件级迭代信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码基准测试设计于智能体时代之前，将模型、框架和环境压缩为单一端到端分数，与智能体软件工程的实际实践不匹配，无法有效指导迭代改进。

Method: 通过分析三类症状来论证现有基准的缺陷：(i) 基准分数混淆了模型与框架其他部分；(ii) 单一参考解的评分方式惩罚同样有效的替代方案；(iii) 缺乏组件级信号导致端到端系统分数难以迭代优化。

Result: 揭示了当前编码基准与智能体软件工程之间的根本性错配，指出框架中任何组件（模型、框架、上下文、环境、反馈信号）的变化都能带来与模型代际更新相当的分数变化。

Conclusion: 现有编码基准需要重新设计，以反映智能体系统的组件化本质，提供可分离、可迭代的评估信号，而非单一的端到端分数。

Abstract: Coding agents have become a major mode of software engineering, but the benchmarks we use to compare them were designed in a pre-agent era: they collapse model, harness, and environment into a single end-to-end score, typically computed against one reference solution, with no component-level signal for iteration. We argue that current coding benchmarks are misaligned with agentic software engineering. A coding agent in practice is not a model: it is a system harness -- a composite of models, harnesses, contexts, environments, and feedback signals, any one of which can move the benchmark score by margins comparable to those between adjacent model generations. We discuss three symptoms: (i) benchmark scores conflate the model with the rest of the harness; (ii) grading against a single reference solution penalises equally valid alternatives; and (iii) the absence of signal at the level of individual harness components makes the end-to-end system score difficult to iterate on.

</details>


### [13] [A Framework for Evaluating Agentic Skills at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17819)
*Maksim Shaposhnikov,Nicolas Fortuin,Simon Stipcich,Maria I. Gorinova,Amy Heineike,Rob Willoughby*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种评估智能体技能（Agent Skills）的框架，允许技能作者构建逼真任务来严格评估技能关键方面，并估算技能效用。作者将该框架应用于500个真实技能，生成了1000个任务，并对19种智能体-模型配置进行了评估。结果显示，模型在遵循技能指令方面差异很大，且技能访问能显著改变模型行为。论文公开了评估数据集以支持未来研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体技能（可复用的结构化知识工件，用于增强LLM智能体能力）已在工业界广泛采用，但其跨领域影响以及在商业与开源模型上的使用尚未得到充分研究，且缺乏可复用的技能评估方法论。

Method: 1. 提出评估框架，允许技能作者构建逼真任务，评估技能关键方面并估算效用。2. 将该方法应用于500个真实技能，从中衍生出1000个任务，并构建了指令遵循与任务完成评分标准。3. 在19种智能体-模型配置（含专有和开源模型）上进行评估。

Result: 1. 不同模型在遵循技能编码的指令方面差异显著，导致性能增益差异大。2. 与无技能设置相比，访问技能会显著改变模型行为，为将工作流编码到LLM智能体中提供了重要机制。

Conclusion: 智能体技能能够有效编码有观点的工作流，不同模型对技能指令的遵循程度存在显著差异，技能访问是改变模型行为的关键手段。公开发布评估数据集以推动后续研究。

Abstract: Agent skills -- structured, reusable knowledge artifacts that augment LLM agent capabilities -- have been rapidly adopted in industry, yet their cross-domain impact and use across commercial and open-source models remain under-studied, and no reusable methodology exists for evaluating an individual skill. In this work, we present an evaluation framework that lets a skill author construct realistic tasks to rigorously assess the aspects of a skill that matter most to them, and that estimates skill utility by solving those tasks. Further, we apply our evaluation approach at scale to 500 real-world skills, generating 1,000 tasks derived from the skills' content, along with instruction-following and goal-completion scoring rubrics. Using these metrics, we evaluate how 19 agent-model configurations, both proprietary and open-source, perform on the tasks. Our results show that models vary widely in how closely they adhere to the instructions encoded in skills, leading to substantial differences in their performance gains. Furthermore, we show that access to a skill significantly changes model behavior compared to the no-skill setup, providing an essential mechanism for encoding opinionated workflows into LLM agents. We release our evaluation dataset to support future work on agent skills.

</details>


### [14] [Planning to Hammer: Difficulty-Aware Decomposition for Automating Rocq Proofs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17981)
*Ning Zhang,Nongyu Di,Zenan Li,Yuan Yao,Xiaoxing Ma*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Quarry是一种基于规划的证据合成框架，通过将证据规划与执行分离，利用LLM提出证据分解策略，再通过基于证据状态难度的排序模型和CoqHammer自动化策略递归证明子引理，在10分钟预算下将成功率提升7%-13%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI生成代码激增使得形式化验证愈发重要，但交互式定理证明器中的机器验证证据生成仍是瓶颈。LLM能提出高层策略但缺乏局部严谨性，自动化策略（如CoqHammer）能可靠处理局部目标但缺乏长远规划能力，需要结合两者优势。

Method: Quarry采用规划-执行分离架构：1）LLM主动提出多个证据分解方案及子引理，在Rocq中临时假设子引理进行类型检查；2）基于证据状态难度模型（估计hammer可解性）对候选方案排序；3）在有界预算内递归证明子引理，将长证据转化为hammer可解的子目标序列。实现基于SerAPI和CoqHammer。

Result: 在使用多个前沿LLM的多个基准测试中，Quarry在10分钟统一预算下，相比最强基线在三个Rocq基准上成功率提升7%-13%。

Conclusion: 可靠的证据自动化可以通过协调神经规划与符号执行来实现，而非取代其中任何一方。

Abstract: As AI-generated code proliferates, formal verification, particularly through interactive theorem provers such as Rocq and Isabelle, becomes increasingly important for ensuring software correctness. However, producing machine-checked proofs in such provers remains a bottleneck. Existing solutions bring complementary strengths to proof automation: large language models (LLMs) can propose high-level proof strategies but lack local rigor, while automated tactics such as CoqHammer can reliably discharge many local goals but lack long-range planning capabilities. To combine the best of both worlds, we present Quarry, a planning-based proof synthesis framework that separates proof planning from proof execution. Specifically, Quarry asks an LLM to actively propose multiple proof decompositions with arbitrary sublemmas, type-checks them in Rocq under temporarily admitted sublemmas, and ranks candidates using a proof-state-based difficulty model that estimates hammer solvability. It then recursively proves sublemmas within a bounded budget, effectively turning long proofs into sequences of hammer-solvable obligations. We implement Quarry on top of SerAPI and CoqHammer and evaluate it using multiple frontier LLMs across multiple benchmarks. The experimental results show that planning-based decomposition with solvability-aware ranking substantially improves automation while maintaining predictable cost. Under a uniform 10-minute wall-clock budget, Quarry improves over the strongest baseline by 7% to 13% in success rate across three Rocq benchmarks. These results demonstrate that reliable proof automation can be achieved by coordinating neural planning with symbolic execution rather than replacing either.

</details>


### [15] [All Smoke, No Alarm: Oracle Signals in Agent-Authored Test Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18168)
*Dipayan Banik,Kowshik Chowdhury,Shazibul Islam Shamim*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过对33,596个AI代理编写的PR中86,156个测试文件补丁的实证研究发现，80.2%的测试补丁包含弱或无显式oracle信号；虽然强oracle的PR原始合并率较低，但回归分析调整后显示强oracle显著提高合并可能性（OR=1.28, p<0.001），表明测试文件计数会高估验证强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码代理在开源PR中越来越多地生成测试代码，但测试文件是否包含有意义的验证逻辑尚不清楚。缺少显式断言的测试文件无法验证行为，基于测试文件存在的质量门控会高估验证强度，因此需要帮助从业者评估代理生成补丁的验证强度。

Method: 对2,807个GitHub仓库中5种编码代理（OpenAI Codex、GitHub Copilot、Devin、Cursor、Claude Code）生成的33,596个代理编写PR中的86,156个测试文件补丁进行实证研究；通过对384个分层补丁的定性分析构建了8种oracle信号类别的句法分类法，并进行大规模定量分析。

Result: 80.2%的测试补丁包含弱或无显式oracle信号；原始合并率方面弱oracle更高，但经代理类型、PR大小、仓库流行度、任务类型和语言调整后，强oracle显著提升合并可能性（OR=1.28, p<0.001）。

Conclusion: 测试文件数量会大幅高估验证强度，从业者应采用oracle感知的质量检查来更准确地评估AI代理生成的代码贡献。

Abstract: Software practitioners increasingly use AI coding agents that generate test code alongside production code in open source pull requests (PRs). Recent studies report more than 932,000 agent-authored PRs across more than 116,000 repositories, yet whether their test files contain meaningful verification logic remains underexplored. Test files lacking explicit assertions execute code without verifying behavior, so quality gates based on test-file presence overestimate verification strength. The goal of this paper is to help practitioners assess the verification strength of agent-authored patches by characterizing oracle signals and their link to merge outcomes and review effort. We conduct an empirical study of 86,156 test-file patches from 33,596 agent-authored PRs across 2,807 GitHub repositories produced by five coding agents: OpenAI Codex, GitHub Copilot, Devin, Cursor, and Claude Code. A qualitative analysis of 384 stratified patches informs a syntactic taxonomy of eight oracle signal categories. Applied at scale, 80.2% of test patches contain weak or no explicit oracle signals. While raw merge rates are lower for strong-oracle PRs, a regression analysis adjusting for agent, PR size, repository popularity, task type, and language shows strong oracles significantly improve merge likelihood (OR = 1.28, p < 0.001). Our findings suggest that test file counts substantially overestimate verification strength and that practitioners can adopt oracle-aware quality checks to more accurately evaluate agent-authored contributions.

</details>

### [16] [Generalization Guarantees for Multi-Input Neural Operator Learning in Sobolev Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17419)
*Yahong Yang,Zecheng Zhang,Wei Zhu,Wenjing Liao,Hao Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对多输入神经算子，在Sobolev范数度量下建立了逼近误差和泛化误差估计，给出了各输入空间对最终误差界的显式贡献，并揭示了在平衡机制下误差率由输入维度、正则性与Sobolev阶数共同决定，模型复杂度依赖呈log-log/log结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经算子理论主要针对单输入函数设定，缺乏对多输入场景（输入函数定义在不同域、不同维度和不同Sobolev正则性下）的理论分析。本文旨在填补这一空白，建立多输入神经算子的逼近与泛化误差理论框架。

Method: 通过将各输入空间的维度、正则性和Sobolev阶数纳入误差界的显式量化，推导了多输入设定下的逼近误差与泛化误差率，并分析了平衡机制下各输入空间贡献的交互作用，模型复杂度的依赖呈log-log/log型结构。

Result: 给出了多输入神经算子在Sobolev范数下的误差界，各输入空间对最终误差的贡献被显式量化；在平衡机制下，误差率由输入维度、正则性和Sobolev阶数的交互决定，模型复杂度具有log-log/log结构。

Conclusion: 本文为多输入算子学习（包括Sobolev训练）提供了统一的理论框架，适用于偏微分方程和科学计算中的算子学习问题。

Abstract: We develop approximation and generalization error estimates for multi-input neural operators, with the output error measured in Sobolev norms. In contrast to standard operator-learning settings with a single input function, our framework allows multiple input functions defined on possibly different domains, with different dimensions and Sobolev regularities. The derived rates explicitly quantify the contribution of each input space to the final error bound. In particular, in the balanced regime, the approximation and generalization rates are governed by the interaction between the input dimensions, regularities, and Sobolev orders, while the dependence on the model complexity retains a \(\log\log/\log\)-type structure. Our analysis provides a general theoretical framework for multi-input operator learning, including Sobolev training, and is applicable to operator learning problems arising from partial differential equations and scientific computing.

</details>


### [17] [Correct When Paired, Wrong When Split: Decoupling and Editing Modality-Specific Neurons in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17057)
*Tingchao Fu,Wenkai Wang,Fanxiao Li,Huadong Zhang,Jinhong Zhang,Dayang Li,Yunyun Dong,Renyang Liu,Wei Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DECODE通过显式解耦和定位多模态大模型中特定模态的神经元群组，解决了编辑解耦失败问题——即多模态输入触发的知识更新无法有效传播到单模态通路，从而确保不同模态触发下的一致性知识更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识编辑范式存在编辑解耦失败问题：多模态输入触发的实体知识更新在单模态输入下会回退到旧知识，模型未能形成统一的实体知识表征。

Method: DECODE方法显式解耦并定位多模态大模型中特定于模态的神经元群组，使知识更新能够同时作用于多模态和单模态通路。

Result: DECODE在不同模态触发条件下均能实现有效的知识更新，缓解了编辑解耦失败现象。

Conclusion: 多模态大模型中的实体知识并非统一存储，而是分散在解耦的模态特定通路中；DECODE通过解耦定位策略有效弥合了多模态与单模态间的更新传播鸿沟。

Abstract: Although Knowledge Editing provides an efficient mechanism for updating the knowledge of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), we find that current paradigms still suffer from an important yet remain underexplored issue : editing decoupling failure, where entity-related knowledge can be updated when the model is triggered by multimodal inputs (text--image query pairs), however, it often reverts to outdated pre-edit facts when the paired inputs are split into unimodal ones. Our in-depth empirical analysis reveals that the entity knowledge in MLLMs is not stored as a unified representation, but is instead distributed across disentangled modality-specific pathways. As a result, updates biased toward multimodal queries fail to propagate effectively to unimodal circuits. To bridge this gap, we propose DECODE, which explicitly disentangles and localizes modality-specific neuron groups for targeted knowledge. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DECODE consistently achieves effective knowledge updates under different modality triggers, thereby mitigating editing decoupling failures.

</details>


### [18] [Operator Boosting Produces Pareto-Efficient PDE Surrogates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17460)
*Lennon J. Shikhman*

TL;DR: 本文提出Operator Boosting框架，通过逐阶段残差学习构建小型神经算子代理，在减少72-95%可训练参数的同时，在30个数据集-架构对中21个获得正向平均精度提升，并在7/10的PDE基准测试中改进经验帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全尺寸神经算子模型作为PDE代理求解器在存储、部署和多查询科学计算工作流中成本高昂，需要一种直接构建紧凑型代理模型的方法，而非先训练大模型再压缩。

Method: 采用逐阶段残差学习框架：从归一化输出坐标的经验均值预测器开始，在残差场上依次训练一系列同族小型神经算子，通过验证集选择的收缩因子整合每个修正项。实例化时使用了FNO、DeepONet和CNO三种架构。

Result: 在30个数据集-架构对中，21个平均精度提升，17个置信区间为正；训练参数减少72-95%；在10个PDE基准测试中的7个（包括2D Navier-Stokes、浅水动力学、达西流、1D传输和反应系统、3D可压缩Navier-Stokes）取得帕累托改进。

Conclusion: Operator Boosting通常能改善神经PDE代理的经验精度-参数帕累托前沿，但也存在残差提升无法抵消压缩损失的PDE和架构依赖的特定情况。

Abstract: Neural operators are widely used as surrogate solution maps for partial differential equations (PDEs), but full-size models can be costly to store, deploy, and evaluate in many-query scientific workflows. This work introduces Operator Boosting, a stagewise residual-learning framework for constructing compact neural-operator surrogates directly, rather than training a large model and compressing it afterward. Starting from the empirical mean predictor in normalized output coordinates, the method trains a sequence of tiny same-family neural operators on residual fields and incorporates each correction through validation-selected shrinkage. We instantiate the framework with Fourier neural operators (FNOs), DeepONets, and convolutional neural operators (CNOs), and compare boosted tiny stacks against full-size monolithic baselines across one-, two-, and three-dimensional PDE benchmarks from PDEBench, APEBench, and The Well. Across 30 dataset-architecture pairs, 21 show positive mean accuracy gains and 17 have positive confidence intervals, while all boosted stacks reduce trainable parameter count by approximately 72-95%. Best-model comparisons show empirical Pareto improvements on 7 of 10 completed PDE benchmarks, including two-dimensional Navier-Stokes, shallow-water dynamics, Darcy flow, one-dimensional transport and reaction systems, and three-dimensional compressible Navier-Stokes. These results show that Operator Boosting often improves the empirical accuracy-parameter Pareto frontier of neural PDE surrogates, while also exposing PDE- and architecture-dependent regimes where residual boosting fails to offset compression.

</details>


### [19] [Diagnosing and Repairing Shape-Prior Shortcuts in Long-Range Single-Shot Fringe Projection Profilometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17093)
*Adam Haroon,Anush Lakshman,Cody Fleming,Beiwen Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对单次曝光条纹投影轮廓术在远距离（1.5-2.1 m）场景下的相位解码失效问题，通过机械可解释性（MI）和共形不确定性量化（UQ）两种诊断方法一致定位到基线UNet依赖物体边界形状先验而非条纹相位解码的故障根源，并提出PhiCalNet架构修复：通过输出包裹相位并接固定可微标定层，从假设空间中移除形状先验解，使物体MAE降低3.3倍至4.46 mm，UQ进一步确认故障位点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远距离单次FPP面临三个未解决挑战：1）反平方强度衰减导致条纹信噪比下降并退化物理真值；2）单帧图像缺乏条纹阶信息，问题本质病态；3）现有架构缺乏机理层面的研究。核心动机是诊断并修复远距离单次FPP的物理失效根源。

Method: 1）诊断阶段：线性探测、Grad-CAM、平面外分布测试三种探针一致发现基线UNet利用物体边界形状先验而非相位解码；2）修复阶段：提出PhiCalNet，输出包裹相位而非深度，通过固定可微标定层（基于物理标定）将相位映射为深度，从架构上（而非损失惩罚）移除形状先验解；3）消融实验：物理信息损失作为软惩罚对深度回归网络无增益，证实架构本身是关键因素；4）验证阶段：共形UQ通过像素快照分歧拒绝顶部5%像素使PhiCalNet RMSE降低64%。

Result: 在15,600张条纹图像、50个物体、1.5-2.1 m距离的光照逼真合成基准上：基线UNet物体MAE为14.54 mm，PhiCalNet降至4.46 mm（3.3倍提升）；残差仅由±π包裹不连续处的0.103%像素贡献；UQ拒绝顶部5%分歧像素后PhiCalNet RMSE从20.6降至7.4 mm（降幅64%），基线仅降3.5%。

Conclusion: MI和UQ作为收敛性诊断工具一致定位故障位点（物体边界形状先验），证实远距离单次FPP的核心失效并非数据或损失层面，而在于网络架构允许了非预期的捷径解；通过架构级修复（从深度回归转为相位回归+物理标定层）可显著提升性能，且UQ可有效识别残差故障像素。

Abstract: Learning-based single-shot fringe projection profilometry (FPP) has been studied mostly at close range. The long-range regime (standoff beyond 1 m) remains largely unaddressed: inverse-square intensity falloff lowers fringe signal-to-noise ratio and degrades physical ground truth, the single-shot problem is ill-posed because fringe-order information is absent from one image, and these architectures have not been studied mechanistically. We present a diagnose-repair-verify study using mechanistic interpretability (MI) and conformal uncertainty quantification (UQ) as convergent diagnostics: they agree on one physical failure locus, driving and verifying an architectural repair. On a photorealistic synthetic benchmark (15,600 fringe images, 50 objects at 1.5-2.1 m), a best UNet baseline reaches 14.54 mm object mean absolute error (MAE). Three probes (linear probing, Grad-CAM, flat-plane out-of-distribution test) converge: the baseline solves the task via object-boundary shape priors rather than fringe-phase decoding. We repair this with PhiCalNet, which outputs wrapped phase rather than depth and applies a fixed differentiable calibration layer mapping phase to depth, removing the shape-prior solution from the hypothesis space architecturally rather than by a loss penalty. A physics-informed loss that enforces the same physics as a soft penalty on a depth-regressing network yields no measurable gain, isolating the architecture as the operative factor. PhiCalNet reduces object MAE 3.3x to 4.46 mm; the residual is carried by 0.103% of pixels at the +/-pi wrap discontinuity. Pixel-wise conformal UQ confirms the diagnosis: rejecting the top 5% of object pixels by snapshot disagreement cuts PhiCalNet RMSE by 64% (20.6->7.4 mm) versus 3.5% for the baseline. MI and UQ converge on the same failure locus.

</details>


### [20] [Informative Missingness to Generate Irregular Clinical Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17106)
*Hadi Mehdizavareh,Gabriele Santangelo,Giovanna Nicora,Simon Lebech Cichosz,Arianna Dagliati,Arijit Khan,Riccardo Bellazzi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于扩散模型的临床时间序列生成方法，能够联合建模实验室检验值及其观测模式（即检验缺失情况）。该方法在MIMIC-III衍生的DACMI基准上验证，通过4小时间隔对齐和7天窗口分段，生成同时包含检验值和观测指示符的轨迹。实验表明生成数据在个体分布和联合嵌入空间上均与真实患者轨迹高度吻合，验证了扩散模型在MNAR缺失机制下捕捉生理学与临床决策之间依赖关系的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子健康记录中的实验室检验因采集时间不规则而存在大量缺失值，且检验缺失本身携带有临床决策和患者生理状态的信息（MNAR缺失机制）。现有方法通常将缺失视为预处理伪影，未能直接建模缺失模式与临床意义之间的关联。本文旨在设计一种能够同时建模连续检验值和离散缺失模式的生成方法，以保留缺失信息对下游任务的潜在价值。

Method: 基于扩散模型框架，扩展TimeDiff架构，引入互补扩散目标函数分别学习连续实验室值和离散缺失模式。将原始图表时间对齐至4小时间隔，并将住院记录分割为7天窗口，每个轨迹对每个时间点的检验值配备对应的观测指示符。采用标准化和归一化等常规变换以稳定训练过程。在公开的DACMI基准（基于MIMIC-III）上评估。

Result: 生成的合成数据在个体实验室分布和联合值-缺失嵌入空间上均与真实患者轨迹高度匹配。实验表明扩散模型能够捕捉MNAR缺失机制下患者生理学与临床测试行为之间具有临床意义的依赖关系。

Conclusion: 该模型可作为构建临床基础模型的初始组件，通过生成保留关键生理-缺失关系的合成先验知识，为后续训练Prior-Data Fitted Networks（使其能够利用信息性缺失）奠定基础。扩展工作将进一步探索该方向。

Abstract: Laboratory tests in electronic health records are collected irregularly, and the absence of a test order can be as informative as the measurement itself. Such missingness reflects clinicians' decisions and patient physiology, making it important to model it directly rather than treat it as a preprocessing artifact. Here we present a diffusion-based approach for generating clinical time series that jointly models laboratory values and their observation patterns using the public Data Analytics Challenge on Missing Data Imputation (DACMI) benchmark derived from MIMIC-III. To preserve realistic sampling, we align chart times into 4-hour intervals and segment admissions into 7-day windows, producing trajectories that pair each lab value with a corresponding observation indicator. Standard transformations and normalization are applied to stabilize training. Our method extends the TimeDiff framework to learn continuous lab values and discrete missingness patterns through complementary diffusion objectives. Experiments show that the generated data closely match real patient trajectories across individual lab distributions and joint value-missingness embeddings, demonstrating that diffusion models can capture clinically meaningful dependencies between patient physiology and clinicians' testing behavior under MNAR-like (missing-not-at-random) missingness. These preliminary results indicate that our model can serve as an initial component toward developing clinical foundation models. By producing synthetic priors that preserve key physiology-missingness relationships, this work motivates the subsequent training of Prior-Data Fitted Networks capable of leveraging informative missingness, which we will investigate in the extended work.

</details>


### [21] [Models Take Notes at Prefill: KV Cache Can Be Editable and Composable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17107)
*Bojie Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Prefix缓存仅对完全共享的前缀有效，本文通过因果分析发现前缀缓存失效的根本原因是模型在预填充阶段已在前缀的"笔记"中写入了结论，而字段自身的key/value向量对决策贡献不足1%。基于此提出可编辑和可组合的缓存机制：通过追加勘误修改笔记，使8B模型在约1%计算量下恢复决策；预编译技能可RoPE重定位后拼接至任意上下文，首次token响应时间从O(L²)降至O(L)。编辑+组合统一代理在保持决策不变前提下实现最高14.9倍延迟降低，vLLM在线基准测试中保持98.5%缓存命中率，p90首次token延迟降低53-398倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Prefix缓存机制要求完全共享前缀，任意字段变更会导致下游缓存全部失效；而直接覆盖字段自身key/value向量并复用其余部分，模型仍基于旧值决策。作者旨在从因果角度解释这一现象，并利用发现的机制实现高效缓存编辑与组合。

Method: 跨四个模型家族（涵盖不同规模、量化、MoE、多模态）进行因果分析，证明模型在预填充阶段已在前缀上下文中写下"结论性笔记"（field-conditioned conclusion），字段自身key/value对决策贡献低于1%。基于"笔记本"隐喻设计：（1）可编辑性：通过追加勘误（erratum）修改笔记内容，结合链式推理（CoT）可在约1%计算量下恢复正确决策；（2）可组合性：笔记具有位置可移植性，预编译技能经过RoPE位置重定位后可拼接至任意上下文，与全量重计算在logits余弦相似度上达到0.90-0.999，首次token时间从O(L²)降至O(L)。最终构建统一编辑+组合代理，并在vLLM在线系统中验证与生产级Prefix缓存的兼容性。

Result: （1）编辑功能：8B模型下仅编辑字段可在约1%计算量下恢复决策（准确率1.00），无CoT时编辑被忽略；（2）组合功能：12个模型上预编译技能拼接后logits余弦相似度0.90-0.999，时间复杂度从O(L²)降至O(L)；（3）统一代理与全量重计算决策一致，延迟降低最高14.9倍；（4）vLLM在线基准中保持98.5%缓存命中率，p90首次token延迟降低53-398倍，且兼容多种注意力变体（通过小型适配器）。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了Prefix缓存失效的根本因果机制——模型在前缀中存储的是已写入结论的"笔记"，而非仅依赖字段自身向量。基于此提出的可编辑、可组合缓存方案通过追加式修改（erratum）和RoPE重定位拼接，在保证决策一致性的前提下大幅提升缓存效率，且与生产级Prefix缓存天然兼容，具有广泛的实用价值。

Abstract: Prefix caching reuses prefill only across an exactly shared prefix, so one changed field invalidates the entire downstream cache. Yet overwriting the field's own key/value vectors and reusing the rest leaves the model acting on the old value. The reason, established causally across four model families: at prefill the model has already written the field-conditioned conclusion onto downstream notes; the field's own key/value drives under 1% of the decision. Read as a notebook of memoized conclusions, two capabilities follow. (1) It is editable. A salient erratum amends the notes; and with chain-of-thought, editing the field alone recovers the decision (1.00 at 8B, ~1% compute), while without CoT it is ignored. (2) It is composable. The notes are position-portable, so a precompiled skill can be RoPE-repositioned and spliced into any context, indistinguishable from full recompute (logit cosine 0.90-0.999, twelve models) at O(L) rather than O(L^2) time-to-first-token. A unified edit+compose agent stays decision-identical to recompute at up to 14.9x lower latency. The approach applies to any per-token attention KV cache, validated across scale, quantization, Mixture-of-Experts, and multimodal caches, and extends to several attention variants through small adapters. Because the erratum is append-only, it composes with production prefix caching: in an online vLLM benchmark it keeps the prefix cache-aligned (98.5% hit-rate), cutting p90 time-to-first-token by 53-398x.

</details>


### [22] [The Critical Role of Model Selection in Causal Inference: A Comparative Analysis of Classification Models within the InferBERT Framework for Pharmacovigilance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17113)
*Csaba Kiss,Roland Molontay,Gabriele Pergola*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了InferBERT框架中不同模型（XGBoost、ALBERT、BioBERT、Med-LLaMA）在因果不良药物事件检测中的表现，发现领域特定的预训练（BioBERT）显著优于简单基线和大规模LLM（Med-LLaMA），校准虽能改善ECE但对准确率影响不一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区分因果性药物不良事件与虚假相关性是药物警戒的核心挑战，InferBERT框架虽整合了Transformer与Do-calculus，但其成功高度依赖底层分类模型的选择，需要系统评估不同模型的影响。

Method: 在Analgesics-induced Acute Liver Failure (AILF) 和 Tramadol-related Mortalities (TRAM) 两个基准上进行比较研究，评估XGBoost、ALBERT（原始InferBERT）、BioBERT（生物医学Transformer）、Med-LLaMA（医疗LLM）四种模型，采用20次重复5折交叉验证，测量准确率、校准误差（ECE）及因果术语与PRR/ROR/EBGM的Jaccard一致性，使用配对t检验检验显著性。

Result: BioBERT在两个数据集上均取得最高准确率；Med-LLaMA尽管规模大且采用参数高效微调，表现却最差；领域特定预训练是决定性因素；校准改善了ECE但对准确率和因果发现的影响不一致；BioBERT与传统药物警戒信号的一致性最强。

Conclusion: 领域特定预训练相比简单基线和大规模LLM具有明显优势，投资于可管理的、领域感知模型对计算药物警戒比简单扩大模型规模更有效。

Abstract: Distinguishing causal adverse drug events (ADEs) from spurious correlations remains a central challenge in pharmacovigilance. The InferBERT framework integrates transformer models with Do-calculus, but its success hinges on the underlying classification model. This study evaluates the impact of model choice in InferBERT, assessing whether simpler models suffice, if domain-specific pre-training helps, whether scaling to LLMs improves causal detection, and the effect of post-hoc calibration. We performed a comparative study on two benchmarks: Analgesics-induced Acute Liver Failure (AILF) and Tramadol-related Mortalities (TRAM). Four models were evaluated-XGBoost (baseline), ALBERT (original InferBERT), BioBERT (biomedical transformer), and Med-LLaMA (medical LLM)-using 5-fold cross-validation repeated over 20 runs. We measured accuracy, Expected Calibration Error (ECE) pre- and post-isotonic regression, and Jaccard concordance of causal terms with PRR, ROR, and EBGM; significance was tested with paired t-tests. BioBERT achieved the highest accuracy on both datasets, while Med-LLaMA underperformed despite its size and parameter-efficient fine-tuning. Domain-specific pre-training was decisive. Calibration improved ECE but had mixed effects on accuracy and causal discovery. BioBERT's superiority also yielded the strongest concordance with traditional pharmacovigilance signals. These results show that domain-specific pre-training provides a clear advantage over simpler baselines and larger LLMs. Investing in manageable, domain-aware models is more effective for computational pharmacovigilance than simply scaling model size.

</details>


### [23] [Probing, Fusion, and Trustworthiness: A Systematic Evaluation of Foundation Model Representations for Multimodal Cancer Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17115)
*Jingyu Hu,Giuseppe Tripodi,Reed Naidoo,Sarah F. McGough,Tapabrata Chakraborti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了基础模型在计算病理学任务中面对分布偏移时的表现，发现图像与组学特征互补，多模态融合在单一模态不占优时有效，且共形预测有助于提升临床推理的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型作为医学数据的强大表示提取器已被广泛认可，但它们在分布偏移下的泛化能力尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 在IH-BC和IH-NSCLC两个真实商业队列上，对五个基础模型进行单模态探测基准测试，比较三种图像-组学融合策略，并利用共形预测评估所选管道的不确定性。

Result: 基础模型在分布外数据上表现具有竞争力；多模态融合仅在单一模态不占主导时带来增益；共形预测显示多数点预测失败时真实诊断仍可被预测集覆盖。

Conclusion: 基础模型表示在分布偏移下表现稳健，不确定性感知推理对临床支持具有重要价值。

Abstract: Foundation models (FMs) have emerged as powerful representation extractors for medical data, yet their generalizability to datasets under distribution shift remains underexplored. This work systematically evaluates FM-based representations on a suite of computational pathology tasks across two real-world commercial cohorts, IH-BC and IH-NSCLC, drawn from the licensed in-house (IH) oncology dataset. The analysis focuses on two modalities, whole-slide images and transcriptomic profiles, drawn from the IH multimodal data. We first benchmark unimodal probing performance across five FMs on eight downstream classification tasks, and find that image and omics representations carry complementary predictive signals. Then we investigate whether multimodal fusion can yield additional gains over unimodal baselines by comparing three image-omics fusion strategies built on paired representations. The trustworthiness of selected unimodal and multimodal pipelines is further assessed through conformal prediction. Our results show that FM representations achieve competitive performance on out-of-distribution data and that multimodal fusion helps mainly when no single modality dominates the signal. Conformal prediction reveals that in the majority of cases where a point prediction fails, the true diagnosis remains recoverable within the prediction set, reinforcing the value of uncertainty-aware inference for clinical support.

</details>


### [24] [MODE: Modality-Decomposed Expert-Level Mixed-Precision Quantization for MoE Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17118)
*Yuanteng Chen,Peisong Wang,Zhilei Liu,Nanxin Zeng,Yuantian Shao,Shiqiang Lang,Tao Liu,Chuangyi Li,Qinghao Hu,Gang Li,Jing Liu,Jian Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MODE提出了一种模态解耦的专家级混合精度量化框架，通过分解模态间的专家选择频率、过滤冗余视觉令牌、并评估各模态的量化敏感性，结合整数线性规划分配位宽，在MoE-MLLM上实现了平均性能损失<2.9%（W3A16）的压缩效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE-MLLM虽性能优异但GPU显存成本过高，现有专家级混合精度量化方法在MoE-MLLM上退化严重，原因是忽略了跨模态和视觉内两个层面的专家重要性估计偏差：视觉令牌的数值主导掩盖了文本模态关键专家，冗余视觉令牌进一步扭曲频率统计。

Method: 提出MODE框架：1) 按模态分解专家选择频率；2) 过滤冗余视觉令牌获得去噪视觉频率；3) 评估各模态量化敏感性作为频率估计的互补信号；4) 将这些信号集成到整数线性规划(ILP)中，在给定预算下为每个专家分配位宽。

Result: 在W3A16设置下平均性能损失控制在2.9%以内，在极端2-bit设置下获得更大增益。

Conclusion: MODE很好地适用于MoE-MLLM的量化压缩，有效解决了现有方法在跨模态和视觉内层面的偏差问题。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts Multimodal Large Language Models (MoE-MLLMs) offer remarkable performance but incur prohibitive GPU memory costs, making compression essential. Among PTQ methods, expert-level mixed-precision quantization has proven effective for MoE-LLMs, yet suffers notable degradation on MoE-MLLMs due to two overlooked biases in expert importance estimation. (1) At the cross-modal level, the numerical dominance of vision tokens causes expert selection frequency to be dominated by vision tokens, masking experts that are critical to the text modality; (2) at the intra-vision level, the large proportion of redundant vision tokens further skew frequency statistics, obscuring experts critical for informative visual content. To bridge gaps, we propose MODE, a modality-decomposed expert-level mixed-precision quantization framework for MoE-MLLMs that decomposes expert selection frequency by modality, filters redundant vision tokens to obtain denoised visual frequency, and further evaluates quantization sensitivity per modality as a complementary signal to frequency-based estimation. These signals are integrated into an Integer Linear Programming formulation to assign per-expert bit-widths under a given budget. Extensive experiments show that MODE is particularly well-suited for MoE-MLLMs, limiting average performance loss to within 2.9% at W3A16, with larger gains at the extreme 2-bit setting.

</details>


### [25] [Noise-Driven Escape from Metastable Phases explains Grokking in Deep Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17120)
*Ibrahim Talha Ersoy,Karoline Wiesner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了深度神经网络中的grokking现象（泛化能力的突然延迟出现）与L2正则化引起的一阶相变中的滞后效应有关。在线性DNN中，通过L2正则化将模型困在亚稳态，SGD噪声驱动其越过能垒后才会逃逸，逃逸时间符合阿伦尼乌斯标度，从而重现了grokking曲线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络具有良好的泛化能力，但grokking现象（长时间过拟合后突然泛化）的起源仍不清楚。本文旨在解释这一现象的机制。

Method: 在线性DNN中，利用L2正则化工程化地使模型陷入亚稳态，研究SGD噪声驱动模型跨越能垒的逃逸过程，并结合稀疏子采样重现典型grokking曲线。

Result: 1) grokking与L2一阶相变中的滞后效应一致；2) 逃逸时间服从阿伦尼乌斯标度；3) 在逃逸时间两个数量级范围内重现了grokking式的延迟收敛；4) 亚稳态的数量等于可学习特征的数量（数据协方差矩阵的奇异值个数）。

Conclusion: grokking现象由L2正则化导致的亚稳态和滞后效应引起，该机制可能也适用于一般非线性DNN，为设计更高效的学习方案提供了路径。

Abstract: Deep neural networks (DNNs) exhibit first order phase transitions under variations of the L2 regularization strength, with each transition marking the onset of a new learnable feature. Below a critical regularization strength, all features are in principle learnable, but coexisting metastable states, separated by energy barriers, can trap the network and impede convergence. A strength of DNNs is their ability to generalize. But many open questions remain, among them the origin of so called grokking: the abrupt, delayed onset of generalization after prolonged apparent overfitting. We show for linear DNNs that grokking is consistent with hysteresis in first-order L2 phase transitions: using L2 regularization to engineer deliberate trapping, we demonstrate that a model in a low-accuracy metastable state escapes only when SGD noise drives it across an energy barrier, with escape times following Arrhenius scaling. We reproduce grokking-like delayed convergence across two orders of magnitude in escape time by deliberately trapping models in metastable phases. Using sparse sub-sampling we also reproduce the canonical grokking curve where test error eventually approaches the final training error. Our work suggests that the number of metastable states equals the number of learnable features -- one per singular value of the data covariance -- the potential for hysteresis grows naturally with task complexity. We provide evidence that the same mechanism likely operates in general nonlinear DNNs. Our results provide routes toward more efficient learning schemes.

</details>

*Rodrigo S. Luna,Thiago H. N. Coelho,Luiz S. L. Neto,Roberto M. Velho,Adriano M. A. Cortes,Renato N. Elias,Alexandre G. Evsukoff,Fernando A. Rochinha,Mauricio Araya-Polo,Herve Gross,Alvaro L. G. A. Coutinho*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数值模拟计算成本高昂，难以满足CO₂地质封存实时监测与快速预测的需求，亟需高效的数据驱动替代模型。

Method: 将计算网格构建为图结构，节点代表网格单元，边编码基于传导率的交互及几何属性；通过各向异性消息传递机制捕捉方向性输运；在潜空间中使用自回归残差公式进行时序建模，并采用多步监督训练。

Result: 模型在SPE11A基准上对气体饱和度和液相密度给出了有竞争力的预测，在长预测窗口下累积误差保持适中水平。

Conclusion: 基于图神经网络的端到端替代模型能够有效复现多相流在复杂地质构造中的关键物理行为，为CO₂地质封存监测提供了高效预测工具。

Abstract: This chapter discusses how a data-driven machine learning approach can reproduce key aspects of the physical behavior of multiphase flows in complex geological formations. We propose an end-to-end graph neural surrogate tailored to CO$_2$ plume migration forecasting in geological storage. The method is evaluated on the SPE11A benchmark, a well-known industry test case designed to assess CO$_2$ storage scenarios and characterized by sharp gas-water interfaces, strong advective transport, and rapid convective mixing with fingering development. The benchmark is reformulated as a graph in which nodes represent computational cells and edges encode transmissibility-based interactions enriched with geometric attributes. Directional transport arising from grid geometry, permeability contrasts, and geological heterogeneity is captured through an anisotropic message-passing mechanism, where interaction weights are computed via geometry-conditioned edge embeddings, biasing message aggregation toward physically relevant transport directions. Temporal evolution is modeled in latent space using an autoregressive residual formulation trained with multi-step supervision. The proposed model produces competitive forecasts of gas saturation and liquid-phase density, which are key indicators for CO$_2$ storage monitoring, with cumulative errors that remain moderate over extended forecasting horizons.

</details>

*Sajjad Khan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对多智能体LLM系统的共享状态并发问题，提出了一个包含四种并发异常（陈旧生成、幻象工具、因果级联、工具效应重排序）的形式化一致性层级（L0-L4），并通过Verus验证工具在Rust运行时中实现了可机械验证的检测与避免机制，在字节跳动deer-flow和LangGraph ToolNode中复现并修复了相关问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统通过内存存储、向量索引和工具注册表共享状态，这种共享本质上是长期运行的读-生成-写操作。现有持久化执行引擎通过确定性重放保证一致性，但缺乏对并发异常的形式化理解和可验证的避免机制。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 使用TLA+形式化四种并发异常（陈旧生成、幻象工具、因果级联、工具效应重排序），建立一致性层级L0⊊...⊊L4；通过Verus开发274个验证义务（零假设、零许可）证明检测器的正确性；在三个已部署的Rust运行时中实现各层级的一致性保证；利用依赖自由预防孪生方法实现L2-L4的exec模式验证。

Result: 三个Rust运行时分别实现了L0-L1（悲观锁、可序列化快照隔离、默认SI）；L2-L4通过exec模式验证实现依赖自由预防（A3、A6、A2在0/1000 vs 1000/1000指标）；L2在三个模型家族的所有120个撤回会话中均预防了A3；在字节跳动deer-flow中复现并修复了静默丢失更新问题；在LangGraph ToolNode中展现并修复了工具效应重排序问题。

Conclusion: 本文贡献在于机械可验证的检测器、精化证明和可实现性工件，而非异常的发现或层级本身（这些是经典结果）。这是首个针对该类运行时的机器检查一致性层级。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems share state through memory stores, vector indices, and tool registries. We model such sharing as long-running read-generate-write operations under deterministic-generation semantics -- the regime durable-execution engines enforce by deterministic replay -- and formalize four concurrency anomalies in TLA+: stale-generation, phantom-tool, causal-cascade, and tool-effect reordering, structural analogues of classical isolation anomalies, each with a TLC counter-example. The exclusion lattice over these anomalies is trivial; the contribution is the mechanically verified realizability and strict separation of one maximal chain within it, $L_0 \subsetneq \cdots \subsetneq L_4$, to our knowledge the first machine-checked consistency hierarchy for such runtimes. A development of 274 Verus obligations (zero assume, zero admit; trust base: two structural axioms and a mutex correspondence) proves the detectors sound and complete against the specifications and each runtime its avoidance set. Three deployed Rust runtimes realize L0-L1 (pessimistic locking, serializable snapshot isolation, default-SI), each verified against stale-generation and refined to its state machine; L2-L4 are exec-mode-verified with dependency-free prevention twins (A3, A6, A2: 0/1000 versus 1000/1000), and L2 is run live across three model families (A3 prevented in all 120 retracted sessions). We reproduce a silent lost update in ByteDance's deer-flow, formalizing its fix as a verified $L_0 \to L_1$ refinement, and exhibit tool-effect reordering in LangGraph's ToolNode on unmodified output, removed by an L3 commit-order sequencer. The verified detector, refinements, and realizability artifacts are the contribution; the phenomena and lattice are classical.

</details>


### [28] [Finsler Geometry, Graph Neural Networks, and You](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17185)
*T. Mitchell Roddenberry,Richard G. Baraniuk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Finsler拉普拉斯算子的图神经网络架构，该架构能够表达各向异性扩散，弥补了传统图拉普拉斯方法仅限于各向同性算子的缺陷，并从理论上证明了离散估计在点云采样下的收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于图拉普拉斯的图神经网络只能近似各向同性的Laplace-Beltrami算子，无法处理各向异性扩散等非线性问题。本文旨在引入Finsler拉普拉斯算子作为非线性替代，构建能表达Finsler几何的图神经网络。

Method: 在流形采样的点云上估计Finsler拉普拉斯算子，证明离散估计随样本增加收敛到真实算子；将该算子表达为图神经网络层，定义受Finsler几何约束的图神经网络族。

Result: Finsler图神经网络能够在实际应用中恢复非线性扩散方程背后的几何结构。

Conclusion: Finsler拉普拉斯算子可有效替代Laplace-Beltrami算子用于图神经网络，使其具备建模各向异性扩散的能力，并为非线性几何深度学习提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Graph neural network architectures based on the graph Laplacian approximate the Laplace-Beltrami operator, thus limiting their application to isotropic operators. As a nonlinear alternative to the Laplace-Beltrami operator, we consider estimates of the Finsler Laplacian on point clouds sampled from a manifold. We prove that these discrete estimates converge to the true operator on the manifold as the number of point samples grows. Moreover, we show that this operator can be expressed as a graph neural network layer, which we use to define a family of Finslerian graph neural networks constrained to express Finsler geometry. We show that Finslerian graph neural networks recover the geometry underlying nonlinear diffusion equations in practice.

</details>


### [29] [Constrained Diffusion Models with Primal-Dual Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17192)
*Samar Hadou,Yigit Berkay Uslu,Alejandro Ribeiro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于原对偶推理的约束扩散模型（PDI），用于求解带平均约束的熵正则化优化问题的最优分布采样。PDI在去噪过程中联合推断最优原始分布及其参数化对偶变量，通过条件分数网络和双重上升更新实现端到端的约束采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有约束扩散模型通常先估计对偶乘子再冻结其值进行采样，这种做法割裂了推断与采样过程。本文旨在实现联合推断，让对偶变量在反向扩散过程中自适应更新以更好地满足约束。

Method: 1) 在拉格朗日对偶域中形式化约束采样问题，最优分布为以最优对偶变量为参数的吉布斯分布；2) 训练一个以对偶变量为条件的单一分数网络，覆盖推理过程中遇到的所有吉布斯分布族；3) 每个反向扩散步先用当前乘子对应的分数场去噪，再根据去噪样本的约束违反程度通过对偶上升更新乘子。

Result: 理论证明了推理轨迹上对偶变量的时间平均收敛到最优对偶变量邻域内，并通过对调度相关的稳定性因子界定了残差对偶失配对终末分布的影响。在高斯混合约束采样、无线资源分配和投资组合管理等任务上验证了有效性。

Conclusion: PDI通过原对偶联合推断实现了约束扩散模型的有效采样，理论保证了收敛性，实验验证了其在多种约束优化问题中的实用性。

Abstract: This paper develops constrained diffusion models with primal-dual inference (PDI) to sample from optimal distributions of entropy-regularized optimization problems with \emph{average} constraints. We formalize constrained sampling in the Lagrangian dual domain, where the optimal distribution takes the form of a Gibbs distribution indexed by the optimal dual variable. Rather than estimating this dual multiplier before sampling and freezing it throughout generation, PDI jointly infers the optimal primal distribution and its parametrizing dual variable. Each reverse diffusion step denoises using the score field associated with the current multiplier and then updates the multiplier through dual ascent using the estimated constraint violation of the denoised samples. To enable this conditional score field, we train a single dual-conditioned score network over the family of Gibbs distributions induced by the dual variables encountered during inference. We prove that the time average of the dual variables generated along the inference trajectory converges to a neighborhood of the dual optimum and bound the effect of residual dual mismatch on the terminal distribution through schedule-dependent stability factors. We evaluate PDI on constrained sampling from a mixture of Gaussians, wireless resource allocation, and portfolio management.

</details>


### [30] [PowerOPD: Stabilizing On-Policy Distillation with Bounded Power Transformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17199)
*Anhao Zhao,Junlong Tong,Yingqi Fan,Ping Nie,Wenjie Li,Xiaoyu Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 标准在线蒸馏（OPD）中使用的逆KL单样本蒙特卡洛估计存在样本效率低、生成不稳定、性能差距大的病理问题，根源在于log-ratio奖励无界导致梯度方差极高且集中于早期位置。本文提出PowerOPD，通过Box-Cox幂变换构造固有有界且符号一致的奖励函数族，在六个数学推理基准和四组Qwen3师生对上实现了平均Avg@8/Pass@8最多提升+6.37/+5.71，同时减少59.2%训练时间和23.1%峰值显存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准在线蒸馏（OPD）在LLM上实际训练时存在严重病理问题：样本效率低、生成动态不稳定、与精确全词表OPD存在巨大性能差距。作者通过奖励级诊断发现，根本原因是log-ratio奖励在构造上无界，导致梯度方差极高且集中在序列早期位置，且标准后处理缩放方法在失真发生后才能作用，效果有限。

Method: 提出PowerOPD，利用Box-Cox幂变换构造一组固有有界且符号一致的奖励函数族，由参数α>0控制。其中log-ratio是α→0的退化极限情况。PowerOPD的奖励函数天然有界，避免了无界奖励导致的梯度方差爆炸问题。

Result: 在六个数学推理基准和四组Qwen3师生对上，PowerOPD相比原始OPD在Avg@8/Pass@8上最高提升+6.37/+5.71；相比后处理稳定方法提升+3.01/+3.54；相比全词表OPD提升+2.59/+8.90。同时减少59.2%训练时间和23.1%峰值显存。较大的α通常能提升准确率、缩短响应长度，并使梯度范数比原始OPD小3000倍以上。

Conclusion: PowerOPD通过构造有界奖励函数有效解决了标准OPD中因无界log-ratio奖励导致的训练病理问题，在性能、效率和稳定性上均显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Standard on-policy distillation (OPD) for large language models estimates the reverse-KL objective using student-sampled tokens, yielding an unbiased single-sample Monte Carlo estimator that avoids vocabulary-wide computation. However, we show that this estimator suffers from severe training pathologies in practice: sample inefficiency, unstable generation dynamics, and a substantial performance gap compared to exact full-vocabulary OPD. Reward-level diagnosis traces these pathologies to the log-ratio reward, which is unbounded by construction, producing extremely high-variance gradients concentrated at early positions and persisting throughout training; standard post-hoc scaling fail as they operate only after this distortion occurs. To solve this problem, we propose PowerOPD: a family of natively bounded, sign-consistent rewards from the Box-Cox power transformation, parameterized by alpha > 0, of which the log-ratio is the degenerate alpha -> 0 limit. Across six mathematical reasoning benchmarks and four Qwen3 teacher-student pairs, PowerOPD achieves benchmark-averaged Avg@8/Pass@8 gains of up to +6.37/+5.71 over vanilla OPD, +3.01/+3.54 over post-hoc stabilization, and +2.59/+8.90 over full-vocabulary OPD, while reducing wall-clock time by 59.2% and peak GPU memory by 23.1%. Larger alpha generally improves accuracy, consistently shortens responses, and keeps gradient norms more than 3,000x smaller than vanilla OPD.

</details>


### [31] [Sum-of-Squares Degree Barriers for the Reweighted-Hinge Method in Robust Halfspace Learning: A Christoffel-Function Characterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17215)
*Xiaoyu Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了异常移除认证的盲区大小由干净边际的Christoffel函数精确刻画，并以此为核心建立了重加权铰链方法（Shen, 2025; Zeng and Shen, 2025）的统整理原则：Sum-of-Squares度决定认证能力，最大可隐藏的腐败质量等于Christoffel函数。由此导出三个关于认证方法（非信息论下界）的紧致结论：边际-度数权衡、度-2异常屏障、以及度-2t算法的前沿追踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 认证方法在移除异常值时存在盲区——低阶矩无法探测的腐败区域。本文旨在精确刻画这一盲区的尺寸，并将其转化为鲁棒学习半空间的统整理原则。

Method: 将Christoffel函数从"检测异常"的正向视角翻转，从攻击者角度解释为"可被有界度认证隐藏的最大腐败质量"；利用重加权铰链方法（reweighted-hinge）框架，以Sum-of-Squares度为资源变量，建立分辨率原理（resolution principle）。

Result: (1) 边际-度数权衡：认证致密pancake至误差ε需要SoS度Ω(log(1/ε))或边际Ω(√log(1/ε)/√d)，阈值2t=Θ((|c|/s)^2)紧致；(2) 度-2异常屏障：度2停滞于η^{1/2}，度4可突破；(3) 度-2t算法追踪前沿η^{1-1/2t}（t=1时恢复Shen 2025结果），增益为显式常数，受pancake密度限制，由度-2屏障证明不可改进。

Conclusion: Christoffel函数从检测工具翻转为攻击资源刻画函数，为认证方法下的鲁棒学习提供了精确的不可改进边界。这些下界是方法论的（certificate method），而非信息论的。

Abstract: A certificate that removes outliers sees the data only through its low-degree moments, and an adversary exploits exactly this, hiding corruption where the clean data already looks typical, in the blind spot no bounded-degree test resolves. That blind spot turns out to have an exact size: the Christoffel function of the clean marginal, the very quantity modern data analysis thresholds to detect outliers, here read from the adversary's side as the corruption a bounded-degree certificate cannot remove. We turn this inversion into the organizing principle of the reweighted-hinge approach to robustly learning $γ$-margin halfspaces under malicious noise (Shen, 2025; Zeng and Shen, 2025): the governing resource is the Sum-of-Squares degree of the outlier-removal certificate, and the resolution principle states that the maximal corruption mass which can hide at a center $c$ from a degree-$2t$ certificate is exactly the Christoffel function $λ_{t+1}(c)$ of the clean marginal. Three consequences follow, all against the certificate method (not information-theoretic). A margin-degree tradeoff: certifying the dense pancake to error $ε$ costs SoS degree $Ω(\log(1/ε))$ or margin $Ω(\sqrt{\log(1/ε)}/\sqrt{d})$, explaining why the $\log(1/ε)$ margin Shen (2025) records is forced, with a weighted-Chebyshev reduction making the threshold $2t=Θ((|c|/s)^2)$ tight modulo one classical weighted-extremal estimate. A degree-$2$ outlier barrier: the resolution principle realized as an explicit instance on which degree $2$ is stuck at $η^{1/2}$ while degree $4$ escapes, locating the method's small breakdown rate in the degree, not the analysis. And a degree-$2t$ algorithm tracing the frontier $η^{1-1/2t}$ (recovering Shen (2025) at $t=1$), whose gain is an explicit constant, capped by the pancake density and shown unimprovable by the degree-$2$ barrier.

</details>


### [32] [Rift: A Conflict Signature for Deception in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17229)
*Petr Nyoma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现，知道真相却故意说谎的模型（ sleeper agent）在内部表征中存在独特的冲突信号（残差秩显著升高2.1-2.3倍），该信号可100%准确区分谎言与诚实错误，且跨模型族、跨语言零样本迁移（AUC高达1.0），同时该信号可检测但不可注入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决ELK（Eliciting Latent Knowledge）的核心难题：传统行为评估无法区分"明知真相却说谎"与"诚实但错误"的模型。需要找到是否存在内部神经签名，能将这两种表面输出相同但认知状态不同的情况区分开。

Method: 通过构造对比实验：sleeper agent（知道真相，在触发条件下说谎）vs naive liar（微调输出相同错误答案但无诚实训练）。分析两者在输出相同错误答案时的残差秩（residual rank），利用残差流中表征秩的差异作为冲突信号度量，并进行跨模型、跨语言、主动隐藏、自我构建谎言等多种条件的验证。

Result: 欺骗的前向传播存在冲突信号：残差秩比naive liar高出2.1-2.3倍。在GPT-2 small/medium（三个种子）和三个指令模型上100%准确区分谎言与诚实。Qwen2.5-1.5B/7B和Phi-3-mini上所有测试事实均验证。Phi-3上谎言与诚实回答及幻觉完美分离（AUC 1.0, p≈6e-11）。信号在自我构建谎言、主动隐藏、长度控制复制下均保持AUC 1.0。跨模型族零样本迁移平均AUC 0.933，跨五语言AUC 1.0。信号只读（可检测不可注入，0/8双向均失败）。

Conclusion: 模型内部存在一种稳健的"知识冲突"神经签名，可以有效区分故意欺骗与诚实错误，该信号具有跨架构、跨语言的可迁移性，且无法被模型主动伪造或注入，为对齐检测提供了可靠的内部监控手段。

Abstract: A model that lies while knowing the truth is the central case ELK cannot handle with behavioral evaluation alone. We ask whether such deception leaves an internal signature distinguishing it from honest error. Our key move is a control for wrongness: we contrast a sleeper agent (knows the truth, lies on trigger) against a naive liar (fine-tuned to emit the same wrong answers with no honest training). Both produce identical wrong outputs; any difference is about knowledge conflict, not incorrectness. We find deceptive forward passes carry a conflict signature - 2.1-2.3x higher residual rank than naive-liar passes on the same wrong answer - strong enough to identify which of two responses is the lie with 100% accuracy and no labels, across GPT-2 small/medium (three seeds) and three instruct models. Across Qwen2.5-1.5B/7B and Phi-3-mini, instructed deception raises residual rank on every tested fact (18/18, 40/40, 34/34); on Phi-3, lies separate perfectly from both honest answers and hallucinations (AUC 1.0, Wilcoxon p~6e-11). The signature survives strategic self-constructed deception (model invents its own lie, AUC 1.0), active concealment attempts (AUC 1.0), and length-controlled replication (20/20, AUC 1.0, p~1e-6). Using basis-free relative representations, a probe trained on one model family detects deception in two other families zero-shot (mean AUC 0.933), surviving simultaneous architecture and format change (AUC 0.821), and transfers across five languages (AUC 1.000, length-controlled). The signature is read-only: detectable but not injectable (0/8 both directions). Honest limitations and six negative experiments are documented in full.

</details>


### [33] [When the Next Step Is Not One Step: Distribution-Aware Execution Modeling for Concurrent Go Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17508)
*Kaviru Hapuarachchi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用并发程序调度非确定性作为训练信号，通过多次运行程序聚合下一事件的经验分布，使用KL散度微调7B模型。在798个真实Go bug（CockroachDB、Kubernetes、gRPC、etcd）的预测任务上，少样本微调达到36.2%准确率，优于Gemini 3.5 Flash零样本（34.8%）和未微调模型（28.6%），并将期望校准误差从0.205降至0.169。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并发程序的调度非确定性导致相同的轨迹前缀可能产生多个有效的下一事件，传统单标签预测模型仅学习随机过程的一个可能结果，无法捕捉这种内在的随机性。

Method: 多次运行每个程序，聚合观测到的下一事件形成经验分布，使用KL散度目标微调7B模型以匹配该分布；同时推导出select阻塞协程的协程泄漏形式化签名。

Result: 分布训练在798个真实Go bug预测任务上准确率36.2%，超过Gemini 3.5 Flash零样本（34.8%）和未微调模型（28.6%）；与交叉熵准确率相当（35.8% vs 36.2%），但期望校准误差从0.205降至0.169。

Conclusion: 将调度非确定性转化为训练信号是有效的，分布训练能更好地校准预测置信度；发布了数据集、适配器和工具。

Abstract: Training a model to predict the next step in a concurrent program is harder than it looks: two runs of the same program from the same trace prefix can produce different next events, both valid, because the scheduler is nondeterministic. A model trained against a single label is learning to guess one outcome of a random process. We turn this around and use the nondeterminism as a training signal. We run each program many times, aggregate the observed next events into an empirical distribution, and fine-tune a 7B model to match that distribution with a KL objective. On 798 held-out predictions drawn from real production Go bugs (CockroachDB, Kubernetes, gRPC, etcd), fine-tuning on fewer than a thousand traces reaches 36.2% accuracy, ahead of Gemini 3.5 Flash used zero-shot (34.8%) and the same model without fine-tuning (28.6%). Distribution training matches cross-entropy on accuracy (35.8% vs. 36.2%) while reducing Expected Calibration Error from 0.205 to 0.169. We also derive a formal goroutine-leak signature for a class of select-blocked goroutines where P(GoUnblock)=0 holds by scheduler semantics, not by learning. We release the dataset, trained adapters, and all tooling.

</details>


### [34] [Uncertainty Quantification of Engineering Structures by Polynomial Chaos Expansion and Multivariate Active Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17233)
*Qitian Lu,Jafar Jafari-Asl,Panagiotis Spyridis,Lukas Novak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多输出工程问题中单一实验设计难以同时满足所有输出精度的问题，提出了一种面向向量值感兴趣量的自适应序贯采样方法，通过平衡输入空间探索与多输出聚合方差利用，在保持数据一致性的同时提高了代理模型的精度和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高保真模型产生多个感兴趣量（QoIs）的工程应用中，单一实验设计（ED）难以同时为所有输出提供充分表征，尤其当不同输出对输入变量表现出不同敏感性时。单独对每个输出采样虽可解决，但会增加采样复杂度和计算成本，且忽略输出间相关性，损害数据一致性。

Method: 将面向标量输出的自适应序贯采样方法推广至向量值QoIs。方法从候选池中根据新样本对输出方差的局部贡献序贯选择样本点，同时平衡基于距离的输入空间探索（exploration）与跨所有输出聚合方差信息的利用（exploitation）。

Result: 通过多个工程问题的数值算例，将所提策略与非序贯的拉丁超立方采样（LHS）进行对比。结果表明，所提方法在提高代理模型精度和稳定性方面表现更优，并能提供更可靠的二阶统计量（方差等）估计。

Conclusion: 提出的自适应序贯采样策略能够有效解决多输出代理建模中单一实验设计不足的问题，在保持数据一致性的同时提高精度和稳定性，为多输出工程问题的代理模型构建提供了更优的采样方案。

Abstract: In many engineering applications, a single high-fidelity model produces multiple quantities of interest (QoIs) under the same input parameters, e.g. finite element models of complex physical systems. To alleviate the high computational cost of direct model evaluations, surrogate models are widely used to construct efficient approximations of model responses. Naturally, the accuracy of surrogates strongly depends on the quality of the experimental design (ED). However, a single ED may not provide an adequate representation for all outputs simultaneously, especially when different outputs exhibit varying sensitivities to the input variables. A straightforward solution is to perform separate sampling for each output, but this results in increased sampling complexity and computational cost. From a statistical perspective, such an approach also ignores potential correlations among all outputs and may compromise data consistency. To address this issue, an adaptive sequential sampling method for constructing polynomial chaos expansion surrogate models is generalized for vector valued QoIs. The method sequentially selects new samples from a candidate pool based on their local contribution to the output variance, while balancing distance-based exploration of the input space and exploitation of aggregated variance information across all outputs. Its performance is compared with non-sequential Latin Hypercube Sampling through several numerical examples from engineering problems. Numerical results demonstrate that the proposed strategy improves both surrogate accuracy and stability, and provides a more reliable estimation of second-order statistics.

</details>
